I  Salute  or  greet  unto  thee5moft  dearly  be-

Smell.  There  is  not  to  me  but  three  viz*  Di-
verfutes  of  Elements,  divers  Proportions,
divers  Decodtions,  and  divers  Mixtions,
Wherefore  if  ye  will  one  Metalline  Body
transform  into another,  ye  muli  know  the
